808 entries, dt-upgrade-readiness to duplicate-clustering. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dt-upgrade-readiness**: Assess a Dynatrace tenant's readiness to migrate off Classic (Gen2) onto the latest Dynatrace (Gen3). Runs ~90 bundled readiness checks directly against the tenant via dtctl (Log Classic, OpenPipeline, classic entity model, IAM/RBAC, service detection, cloud integrations, ActiveGate/network, Synthetic, DEM, App Security, classic apps), ranks blockers by impact, and generates a self-contained HTML report. Read-only. Triggers: "upgrade readiness", "Gen2 to Gen3", "Gen3 migration", "ready to upgrade", "migrate off classic", "upgrade blockers", "what's blocking the upgrade". ([dakota-hanson-dynatrace/d-team-skills](https://github.com/dakota-hanson-dynatrace/d-team-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dt-upgrade-readiness))

- **duanxianxia-pool**: 短线侠股票池维护（3天3板池）与梯队角色标注：build 重建股票池 / show 查看 / check 主线持续提醒 / roles 梯队角色（连板/断板/分歧/观察 + 昨日量能）写入 ~/.cache/duanxianxia/pool.json，供次日盘前读取。 含同概念比选规则（连板&gt;断板&gt;连续分歧；唯一连板+昨日放量=概念温度计）。脚本 scripts/pool_builder.py 仅依赖 requests、内置 ≥10 秒限流防整站 403。 使用时机（即使未明说也应触发）：盘后 18:50 池维护（深查第一步）、盘前取池内最强票、 用户问「股票池 / 梯队角色 / roles / 主线持续提醒」时。 ([627-hub/duanxianxia-skill](https://github.com/627-hub/duanxianxia-skill/tree/HEAD/references/pool))
